'왜 이 원고가 X로 나오지', '이 항목이 안 잡혀', '오탐 원인 분석', '미탐 원인', 'batch_compare 결과 분석', '대조.md 봐줘', '원본 자체 X 가려줘'처럼 판정이 기대와 다른 이유를 묻는 요청이면 반드시 이 스킬을 사용할 것. 코드 수정까지 원하면 f02-fix-loop이 이 스킬을 포함해 진행한다. 미구현 항목의 신규 설계, 화면 문제, 단위 테스트 실패 디버깅에는 쓰지 않는다. - **f02-fix-loop**: F02 검수 엔진의 오판정을 고치는 전체 루프(원인 분석 → 규칙 수정 → 판정 검증)를 에이전트 3명으로 조율하는 오케스트레이터. '이 오탐 고쳐줘', '미탐 수정해줘', '이 원고 차례 ③이 X로 나오는데 고쳐줘', '대조 결과의 미탐·오탐 처리해줘', '배치 결과 보고 규칙 고쳐줘', '판단불가가 너무 많이 나와', '오판정 수정'처럼 검수 판정이 기대와 달라 엔진을 고쳐야 하는 요청이면 반드시 이 스킬을 사용할 것. 후속 작업도 이 스킬로 처리한다: '다시 돌려줘', '재실행', '검증만 다시', '분석만 다시', '사양 수정해서 다시 구현', '이전 결과 기반으로 보완', 'QA 반려된 것 고쳐줘', '판정 변화 표 업데이트'. 원인만 궁금하면 f02-case-triage, 직접 고친 코드의 검증만 원하면 f02-verdict-verify를 쓴다. 미구현 항목의 신규 구현, 웹 화면·API·DB 변경에는 쓰지 않는다. ([z1-won/ncs-web-test](https://github.com/z1-won/ncs-web-test/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/f02-fix-loop))
- **f02-rule-fix**: F02 검수 엔진의 규칙·파서·기준 데이터를 수정 사양대로 고치는 구현 절차. 수정 위치 고르기, 합성 입력 단위 테스트, 판정·비고 작성 규칙, 골든 재생성 판단, 문서 갱신을 다룬다. '규칙 고쳐줘', '이 오탐 수정', '파서 수정', '행동명사 목록 보정', '사양대로 구현'처럼 src/ncs_core 또는 src/ncs_* 엔진의 판정 로직을 바꾸는 작업이면 반드시 이 스킬을 사용할 것. 원인 분석부터 필요하면 f02-fix-loop을 쓴다. 웹 화면(frontend)·API(ncs_web)·DB 변경에는 쓰지 않는다. ([z1-won/ncs-web-test](https://github.com/z1-won/ncs-web-test/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/f02-rule-fix))

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/f1-score-across-tools-comparison))
- **f1-score-benchmark-comparison**: Use when comparing a generalized model trained on merged multi-dataset training data against disease-specific counterparts on each cancer type's validation set, using macro-averaged F1 deltas. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/f1-score-benchmark-comparison))
- **f1-score-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when evaluating annotation or classification accuracy of single-cell labels — e.g. Census predictions vs original cluster-level labels — across perturbations such as cell-number down-sampling (5–500 cells per cluster), producing F1 distributions and non-parametric significance tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/f1-score-benchmark-evaluation))
- **f1-score-benchmark-reproduction**: Use when the goal is to verify or benchmark a machine-learning classification pipeline against published per-class F1-scores on SDSS+WISE photometry, or to establish a reproducible galaxy/QSO/star classification baseline before modifying the method. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/f1-score-benchmark-reproduction))
- **f1-score-benchmarking**: Use when comparing cell annotation algorithms (e.g., CellO IR/CLR, 1NN, scMatch, SingleR) on a labeled single-cell dataset, or when testing whether classification performance is stable across parameter settings such as Leiden clustering resolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/f1-score-benchmarking))
- **f1-score-computation**: Use when benchmarking read classifiers in a mock-community depletion setting: you have per-read target/non-target calls from SPUMONI or minimap2 over multiple index strategies and need F1 scores comparable to the paper's Table 1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/f1-score-computation))
- **f1-score-computation-across-annotation-levels**: Use when comparing predicted genome annotations (GTF) from multiple gene-prediction pipelines against a reference GTF and you need multi-level precision/recall/F1 statistics to decide which pipeline performed . ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/f1-score-computation-across-annotation-levels))
- **f1-score-computation-across-coverage-levels**: Use when benchmarking SV callers on downsampled PacBio HiFi BAMs (e.g. 5x, 10x, 20x, 34x) to quantify how precision/recall/F1 for deletions and insertions &gt;50 bp varies with coverage against a truth set like GIAB HG002. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/f1-score-computation-across-coverage-levels))
- **f1-score-computation-across-dropout-levels**: Use when benchmarking a pretrained Pollock module's tolerance to artificial sequencing dropout — e.g., you have .h5ad/.rds validation data with ground-truth 'cell_type' annotations and need an F1-versus-dropout robustness curve. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/f1-score-computation-across-dropout-levels))
- **f1-score-computation-against-ground-truth-labels**: Use when comparing automatic malignant-vs-non-malignant classification methods on single-cell count matrices that come with known tumor/normal ground-truth labels, and you need a single harmonic-mean accuracy number per condition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/f1-score-computation-against-ground-truth-labels))
- **f1-score-computation-variant-calling**: Use when evaluating SNV callers (e.g. miniSNV, Longshot, Clair3) on real or simulated ONT data: you have caller VCFs and truth variant sets and need per-caller, per-condition precision, recall, and F1 values. - **f1-score-cross-method-comparison**: Use when accuracy rankings of SV callers (e.g. sawfish vs Sniffles2 vs pbsv on 32x HG002 HiFi WGS) must be confirmed with an independent evaluator instead of only Truvari 4.2.2, via precision/recall/F1 against a GIAB T2T SV benchmark. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/f1-score-cross-method-comparison))
- **f1-score-evaluation-of-rare-cell-calls**: Use when benchmarking rare-cell or doublet detection on subsampled scRNA-seq data with known planted cells, to compare methods like GapClust, RaceID, GiniClust, CellSIUS, and FiRE by F1 score. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/f1-score-evaluation-of-rare-cell-calls))
- **f1-score-extraction-and-method-comparison**: Use when a compound-identification run over 2D 1H,13C HSQC spectra must be scored and compared across methods (MCF grid, MCF-D peak-list variant, MetaboMiner baseline) using F1 metrics. - **f1-score-model-comparison**: Use when comparing a novelty detection model (e.g. GoFAE-DND) against other anomaly detectors and supervised classifiers using F1 scores across imbalanced class ratios of simulated data.
